# Regulatory Watch — Cross-Country Reform Tracker

A single date-stamped registry of property-relevant reforms, transposition deadlines, ENDED programs, and watchlist items. Maintained as the **single source of truth for "what changed when"** — playbooks reference this; this references playbooks.

**Why it exists**: At 126 countries × 41 sections, the highest-blast-radius hallucination is asserting that an ENDED program is still active or quoting a tax rate that was reformed last quarter. This file makes "did anything change?" a single-file lookup before any re-stamp.

**Maintainer**: human + Claude. Each entry must have a primary-source URL, a verified date, and a next-revisit date.

---

## How to use this file

### Before producing any output

`--tax`, `--rental`, `--visa`, `--finance`, `--mains` (where regulated) sections must consult this file. If a relevant entry's `revisit_by` date has passed AND the playbook still asserts the pre-reform value, the section's confidence auto-downgrades (see `updater.md` § Auto-downgrade).

### Before re-stamping a section's `**Last verified**`

Check this file for entries touching the section since the prior stamp. If a reform is logged here that the playbook doesn't reflect, the section is **not yet re-verified** — patch the playbook first.

### When adding an entry

Required fields:
- `country` (ISO2)
- `topic` (one of: tax, rental, visa, finance, ownership, esg, cadastre, eurozone, other)
- `effective_date` (YYYY-MM-DD or "pending" + estimate)
- `enacted_date` (when the law/regulation was passed)
- `verified_date` (last time we confirmed status)
- `revisit_by` (next mandatory check)
- `source_url` (primary government / official journal preferred)
- `impact_tier` (1–4, see below)
- `playbook_sections_touched` (list of `--<flag>` flags)

---

## Impact tiers — how to rate a reform

The tier drives revisit cadence and anti-hallucination priority.

| Tier | Meaning | Revisit cadence | Example |
|---|---|---|---|
| **1 — CRITICAL** | Reverses a prior assertion the skill makes (e.g., visa program ENDED, tax rate inverted, ownership opened/closed). Stale claim is a confident lie. | 90 days — full re-research from primary sources, not just a figure check | MT MEIN ECJ ENDED 29 Apr 2025 |
| **2 — MAJOR** | Material number change (>20% rate move), new mandatory diagnostic, EU directive transposition affecting many countries. | 90 days | RO VAT 19 → 21% Aug 2025; EPBD 2024/1275 |
| **3 — MEANINGFUL** | Threshold/credit/exemption tweak; parliamentary stage advance; rumored-to-draft transition. | 180 days | CY 2026 Comprehensive Tax Reform (CGT exemptions raised) |
| **4 — INCREMENTAL** | Indexation adjustment, fee tweak, technical correction, watchlist signal. | 365 days | LU Bëllegen Akt indexation; LV cadastral value rebase rumored |

## ENDED programs — anti-hallucination critical (Tier 1)

Every entry below is **CONFIRMED CLOSED**. Listing any of these as active is the highest-stakes failure mode for `--visa`. Cross-check before any visa-section output.

| Country | Program | Closed date | Source / mechanism | Successor (if any) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 🇲🇹 MT | MEIN (Investor citizenship) | 29 Apr 2025 | ECJ Case C-181/23 ruling; investment route repealed by Act XXI of 2025 (in force 24 Jul 2025) | none (art. 10(9) citizenship by merit, LN 159 of 2025, is not an investment route) |
| 🇪🇸 ES | Golden Visa | 03 Apr 2025 | Law 1/2025 (Justice efficiency act) | none |
| 🇮🇪 IE | IIP (Immigrant Investor Programme) | 15 Feb 2023 | Government press release | none |
| 🇨🇾 CY | Citizenship by Investment | 01 Nov 2020 | Council of Ministers decision | non-dom 17yr (residency only) |
| 🇧🇬 BG | CBI / fast-track | 18 Jun 2024 | Bulgarian parliament | none |
| 🇭🇺 HU | Residency Bond | 31 Mar 2017 | Programme expiration | Guest Investor 2024 (different) |
| 🇬🇧 UK | Tier 1 Investor | 17 Feb 2022 | Home Office | none (Innovator Founder different) |
| 🇦🇺 AU | Significant Investor Visa (SIV / 188C) | 31 Jul 2024 | Migration program 2024-25 | none |
| 🇵🇹 PT | NHR (original 10-yr scheme) | 31 Dec 2023 (last applications) | OE 2024 | IFICI / NHR 2.0 — narrower, **pensions excluded** |
| 🇵🇹 PT | Golden Visa — ALL real-estate routes (residential + commercial) | 07 Oct 2023 | Lei 56/2023 (Mais Habitação) | GV via non-real-estate funds / research / culture / company + jobs only |
| 🇲🇪 ME | Citizenship by Investment | 31 Dec 2022 | Programme expiration | none |
| 🇦🇱 AL | CBI (proposed but never operational) | n/a | Never enacted | n/a — do not list as available |

**Rule**: If a user asks "what visa programs are open in <country>", consult this table first; only entries NOT here are candidates for "open" status, and even those need a fresh `verified_date` ≤180 days.

- `2025-09-22 | rental | Madrid STR freeze SUPERSEDED by a permanent zoning regime — do not describe Madrid as frozen. MADRID: the April 2024 licence freeze was an express art. 70 LSCM cautionary measure ("Suspensión temporal de nuevas licencias ... es una medida cautelar adoptada antes de que se defina la futura ordenación de las VUT", Ayuntamiento de Madrid), and art. 70.4 LSCM caps such a suspension at "no podrá exceder de un año", extendable one further year — it cannot still be running in 2026. It was replaced by the Modificación del PGOU "para la mejora del uso residencial" (expediente 135-2024-00206, Plan RESIDE), definitively approved by Acuerdo del Consejo de Gobierno de la Comunidad de Madrid of 27 Aug 2025 (BOCM 211 of 4 Sep 2025); normative text in BOCM 226 of 22 Sep 2025, which the Ayuntamiento transparency portal labels normativa aplicable. New art. 7.6.3 bis binds ONLY the APE 00.01 Centro Histórico (ámbito per art. 4.3.17). Inside it: hospedaje in part of an EXISTING RESIDENTIAL building is barred on every floor (para. 10.b.i "No se admite su implantación en ninguna de las plantas del edificio") — dispersed VUT in central residential buildings is permanently barred, not paused; in existing NON-residential buildings hospedaje stays admissible as a complementary use (any situation at levels A and B, upper floors only at C and D, para. 10.a). Edificio exclusivo then varies by the art. 8.1.29 use-compatibility level — levels A and B (para. 8): on parcels with an existing non-residential building plain "uso alternativo" (plan especial de protección if catalogued level 1 or 2); on unbuilt parcels or existing residential buildings VUT is "uso alternativo" only in buildings catalogued protection level 3, the activity licence lapses after "quince (15) años" back to vivienda colectiva and that condition "se harán constar en el Registro de la Propiedad", autorizable at protection levels 1 or 2 via plan especial, and "Uso prohibido en parcelas no edificadas y en edificios no catalogados"; levels C and D (para. 9): plain "uso alternativo", plan especial only if catalogued level 1 or 2, with NO 15-year lapse and NO Registro annotation; level E (para. 11) and Colonias Históricas (para. 6.b): hospedaje not admitted. Para. 4: art. 7.6.3 bis does not apply to parcels holding a validated Estudio de Repercusión por Implantación de Usos or a definitively approved Plan Especial for hospedaje not yet implemented, while those instruments stay in force; para. 7: the conditions refer to parcels whose uso cualificado is residential. Áreas de Planeamiento (API, APE, APR) interior to the APE take the A and B conditions (para. 6.a). The level map sits in art. 8.1.29, which BOCM 226 does NOT republish — establish the parcel's level before relying on any of the above. Citywide art. 7.6.3.4: locales in residential buildings "contarán siempre con acceso independiente". Disposición derogatoria única folds the 2019 Plan Especial de Hospedaje (PE 00/313, approved 27 Mar 2019, BOCM 23-04-2019) into art. 7.6.3 bis. Litigation: no primary court source checked — press only (news-only, lower confidence): TSJM admitted a PSOE contentious appeal (reported Oct 2025) and, by auto dated 4 Mar 2026, refused the interim suspension sought by a VUT operator (El País, 9 Mar 2026); no merits ruling located at 2026-10-21 — treat annulment risk as unassessed. BARCELONA: unchanged — PEUAT "estableix el creixement zero dels HUT a tota la ciutat", but a cessation in a saturated zone "es permetrà una nova alta o la redistribució a la zona de creixement contingut", so new HUTs are not absolutely impossible citywide; per-zone detail lives in the BCN November 2028 HUT-expiry entry (no calendar day is sourced) (2026 data, Ajuntament de Barcelona PEUAT page consulted 2026-10-21) | BOCM num. 226 de 22 Sep 2025 anuncio 47 https://www.bocm.es/boletin/CM_Orden_BOCM/2025/09/22/BOCM-20250922-47.PDF + Ayuntamiento de Madrid Portal de Transparencia expediente 135-2024-00206 + art. 70 LSCM consolidado BOE-A-2001-18984 https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2001-18984 + Ajuntament de Barcelona PEUAT https://ajuntament.barcelona.cat/urbanisme-accio-climatica-mobilitat-pla-barris-serveis-urbans/ca/pla-especial-urbanistic-dallotjaments-turistics + El País 9 Mar 2026 (TSJM auto on interim measures, news-only) | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 1 | --rental (ES)`

- `2019-03-27 (norms absorbed into art. 7.6.3 bis by BOCM 22 Sep 2025) | rental | Madrid PEH — legacy entry CORRECTED and NARROWED; two limbs WITHDRAWN as unsourced. DATE and SCOPE were wrong: the Plan Especial de Hospedaje is a 2019 instrument covering ELEVEN districts, not a 2018 Distrito Centro plan. Disposición derogatoria única of BOCM 226 of 22 Sep 2025 anuncio 47 names it verbatim "Plan Especial de Regulación del Uso de Servicios Terciarios en la clase de Hospedaje. Distritos de Centro, Arganzuela, Retiro, Salamanca, Chamartín, Tetuán, Chamberí, Moncloa-Aravaca, Latina, Carabanchel y Usera, aprobado definitivamente el 27 de marzo de 2019 y publicado en el BOCM el 23-04-2019 (PE 00/313)" — that eleven-district list is the REPEALED plan's scope, NOT a current perimeter. NOT A LIVE INSTRUMENT: its norms "quedan incorporadas, con las adaptaciones necesarias, en el texto del artículo 7.6.3 bis de las Normas Urbanísticas del PG97" and "Quedan sin efecto los documentos de Diagnóstico y Memoria del citado Plan Especial" — never cite the PEH as current. The operative regime is the 2025-09-22 Madrid entry above and is deliberately NOT restated here; note only that art. 7.6.3 bis.2 fixes its own ámbito as "el del Área de Planeamiento Específico del Centro Histórico (APE 00.01) definido en el artículo 4.3.17" and art. 4.3.17 is not reproduced in this anuncio, so the perimeter cannot be resolved from it — the string Distrito Centro does not appear in the anuncio at all. WITHDRAWN as unsourced: ">90 nights/yr classed as commercial use". Full-text searches of both instruments run 2026-08-07 find no nights-per-year or days-per-year threshold: in the anuncio, días, noches, al año and pernocta are absent and the single occurrence of 90 is "unidades menores de 90 m² útiles"; in Decreto 79/2014 as amended by DECRETO 27/2026 de 25 de marzo (BOCM 80 of 6 Abr 2026, "entrará en vigor a los veinte días siguientes al de su publicación", so in force since late April 2026) al año, anuales and noventa are absent from both texts. The only period rule in the 2014 original was a per-stay floor in old art. 17.3 ("no podrán contratarse por un período inferior a cinco días"), which is not a per-year cap and which the 2026 decree does not carry forward when it rewrites art. 17 wholesale ("Nueve. El artículo 17 queda redactado del siguiente modo"). The 2026 decree instead empowers municipalities to limit "el número máximo de viviendas de uso turístico por edificio, al ámbito, zona, sector o períodos", so any day-count could only sit in a municipal limit adopted under that delegation — data not publicly available in the sources checked; verify at Ayuntamiento de Madrid and the Comunidad de Madrid dirección general competente en materia de Turismo. ALSO WITHDRAWN: "portal/lift separation from residents" — portal does not occur in the anuncio and the single ascensor occurrence is an unrelated rehabilitation rule ("obras de acondicionamiento y/o de reestructuración puntual para implantación de ascensores"). What IS sourced is weaker and citywide, not Distrito-Centro-specific: art. 7.6.3.4 "Los locales integrados en edificios de uso residencial, con independencia de su modalidad y situación, contarán siempre con acceso independiente". The stronger wording "acceso independiente desde el exterior, sin utilizar elementos comunes de circulación del edificio" is art. 7.1.4 apartado 4 and is expressly displaced for this use class — "Las condiciones de acceso independiente de los locales de la clase de uso terciario hospedaje serán las establecidas en el artículo 7.6.3.4 de estas normas" — so do not attribute a common-stair or lift exclusion to hospedaje. TIER LOWERED 2 to 3: with both limbs withdrawn this line no longer records a reform and no longer reverses a live regime assertion (the supersession is carried by the 2025-09-22 Tier-1 entry); what remains is a date and scope correction plus two withdrawals. ACTION: shared/rental-yield-delta.md line 90 repeats the withdrawn wording and line 51 lists MAD Distrito Centro PEH in the zone-freeze list — patch both | BOCM num. 226 de 22 Sep 2025 anuncio 47 https://www.bocm.es/boletin/CM_Orden_BOCM/2025/09/22/BOCM-20250922-47.PDF + DECRETO 27/2026 de 25 de marzo BOCM num. 80 de 6 Abr 2026 https://www.bocm.es/boletin/CM_Orden_BOCM/2026/04/06/BOCM-20260406-1.PDF + Decreto 79/2014 de 10 de julio BOCM num. 180 de 31 Jul 2014 https://www.bocm.es/boletin/CM_Orden_BOCM/2014/07/31/BOCM-20140731-1.PDF | 2026-08-07 | 2027-02-03 | 3 | --rental (ES)`

### 🇮🇪 IE

- `2026-03-01 | rental | RPZ replaced by permanent nationwide rent control. Residential Tenancies (Amendment) Act 2025 (No. 5 of 2025, signed 19 Jun 2025, in force the day after passing) deemed every non-RPZ area an RPZ from 20 Jun 2025 to 28 Feb 2026. Residential Tenancies (Miscellaneous Provisions) Act 2026 (No. 3 of 2026, signed 24 Feb 2026; rent and termination sections commenced 1 Mar 2026 by S.I. 67/2026) repealed RPZ sections 24A-24C and caps annual increases on all private + student-specific tenancies at 2 percent or CPI whichever lower (AHB and cost-rental tenancies are outside national rent control per RTB). CORRECTION: the new-build carve-out (CPI-linked only, no 2 percent cap) turns on a commencement notice or 7-day notice lodged on or after 10 Jun 2025, not on construction start, and covers apartment complexes and student-specific accommodation buildings, plus extensions of 25 percent or more of floor area and change-of-use conversions (RTA s.19(4)(aa)). New tenancies from 1 Mar 2026 run as 6-year tenancies of minimum duration; market reset only at a new tenancy where the previous one ended by tenant choice, tenant breach or the dwelling no longer suiting the tenant (not after a no-fault termination), or at the end of a 6-year cycle (student-specific: 3-year cycle). Landlords with more than 3 tenancies, and companies, cannot end post-1 Mar 2026 Part 4 tenancies on sale, own or family use, substantial refurbishment or change-of-use grounds (RTA s.35B); RTB: any landlord may end a tenancy in its first six months for any reason. Residence is the Order's own test, not income-tax residence: present on the Island at least 183 days in the 364-day window ending on the transaction date, or a declaration before the transaction of intent to become resident within 183 days (arts 4-5); a single resident co-buyer moves a joint purchase from the non-resident to the non-owner-occupier rate, and wholly or partly commercial property pays the non-owner-occupier rate (arts 9-10); family-member co-buyers get the owner-occupier rate on the qualifying share, capped at 75% of value (art 10). Still the Order in force per the gov.im Land Registry page (2023 Order WEF 01 May 2023; online fee calculator from 1 May 2023); no amending or replacement order found. - `2026-07-15 | visa | EU-UK Gibraltar Treaty signed at Brussels 14 Jul 2026 and PROVISIONALLY APPLIED since 15 Jul 2026 (Art. 336(2): conditional on the implementation plan and administrative arrangements being in effect, published 14 Jul 2026); NOT yet in force or ratified — Art. 336(1): entry into force on the first day of the month after both Parties notify completion of internal procedures; EU side: Commission submitted it to the European Parliament for consent 14 Jul 2026, procedure 2026/0058(NLE) joint AFET/INTA, awaiting committee decision, indicative plenary 14 Dec 2026 (OEIL), then a Council conclusion decision; UK CRAG: laid 14 Jul 2026, first 21-sitting-day objection period ends 22 Oct 2026 (Commons Library CBP-10980). Art. 28 removes all physical barriers to the circulation of persons; Art. 29 puts border checks at Gibraltar port and airport (entry: Gibraltar authorities then Spain; exit: Spain then Gibraltar), and there are no immigration controls at the Gibraltar-Spain land border (HMGoG Technical Notice 778/2026). Art. 42: persons resident in Gibraltar (legal right to reside, any nationality; EU/EEA/Swiss citizens fall outside the definition) get no entry/exit stamps and are exempt from EES and ETIAS — in practice some residents were wrongly registered in EES after 15 Jul 2026; the Commission has issued further instructions to Schengen authorities and residents may request erasure from the Schengen State of their last recorded crossing (HMGoG Technical Notices 696/2026 of 23 Sep 2026 and 778/2026 of 13 Oct 2026). Residence: the treaty gives no right of establishment and residence stays under Gibraltar's own immigration laws (HMGoG PR 148/2026); Art. 45 proves the right to reside by a Gibraltar identity card or residence permit, a residence permit needs a genuine connection (actual and regular physical presence or other objective and verifiable criteria), and Art. 45(6) bars meeting that condition through predetermined investments in Gibraltar's economy or real estate or predetermined payments to the Gibraltar authorities; Art. 50: before issuing or renewing a residence permit Gibraltar notifies Spain, which has 28 days (extendable by 14) to object on public policy, internal security, public health or international relations grounds (including a SIS alert), silence counting as no objection; proposed changes to the residence conditions go to the Cooperation Council for an opinion (Art. 45(4)). Domestic residence rules are the Residency Regulations 2026 (see Residence Criteria entry) | UK-EU Agreement MS No.9/2026 vol I (gov.uk) + gov.uk treaty collection + OEIL 2026/0058(NLE) + HMGoG PR 148/2026 + Technical Notices 696/2026 and 778/2026 + Commons Library CBP-10980 — https://commonslibrary.parliament.uk/research-briefings/cbp-10980 | 2026-10-21 | 2026-12-15 | 1 | --visa --foreign-buyer --remote (GI)`
- `2026-07-14 | visa | Residence Criteria framework (announced HMGoG PR 466/2026, 17 Jun 2026: residence based on genuine economic contribution and regulatory compliance; residence alone does not automatically give access to wider social benefits or services reserved for Gibraltarian Status) IN FORCE as the Residency Regulations 2026 — Legal Notice 166 of 2026 (Gazette No. 5323, 9 Jul 2026; made under ss.13 + 43(7) of the Treaty on Gibraltar and the European Union Act 2026; corrigendum LN 175/2026 of 10 Jul 2026 fixes two typos only; no amending instrument on gibraltarlaws.gov.gi as of 2026-10-21), in operation 14 Jul 2026. Routes: employee, self-employed, student, Crown/HM forces, existing resident (IARA residence document + civilian registration card + 6 months' continuous lawful residence), permanent resident, plus Government discretion (reg. 15: good character + interests of Gibraltar, e.g. wider economic or reputational benefit without material demand on public resources); identity-card holders need no permit; no nationality criterion (HMGoG PR 682/2026). Employee and self-employed applicants must earn at least Gibraltar average gross annual earnings, be 55 or under (Chief Minister may waive either for important skills or a shortage sector; under-30 employee earnings waiver against an employer deposit) and show a purchase or rental agreement for a Gibraltar property used as their primary residence (may be conditional on the permit; not a vessel, not a holiday rental, rentals at least 12 months, bona fide). Permits last 1 year, renewable; fee £250, renewal £20 per person; £2,500 administrative penalty for residing without a valid permit; refusal where the Schengen authorities object (reg. 20); IARA ss.18-34 and Part IV repealed, Withdrawal Agreement rights saved. ANNOUNCED, NOT YET MADE: HMGoG PR 813/2026 (21 Oct 2026) says the Regulations will be amended to add a route for entirely self-sufficient people over 55 (private medical insurance, a financial contribution to the health system, at least six months a year in Gibraltar), to be published by end-October 2026 after consultation. Cat-2: HMGoG PR 469/2026 (18 Jun 2026) announced the fee rising from £1,233 to £5,000 and new-applicant minimum net wealth rising from £2M to £5M, with existing holders grandfathered and loss of the right to remain resident for a person who does not keep Cat-2 status. Only the fee is legislated: Qualifying (Category 2) Individuals (Amendment) Rules 2026, LN 127/2026 (Gazette No. 5316, 22 Jun 2026, in operation on publication), substitutes £5,000 for £1,233 in rule 8(1)(a) and changes nothing else. The consolidated Rules (22 Jun 2026) set no numeric net-worth figure (rule 6(a): substantial and sound financial standing), so the £5M is an administrative threshold per PR 469 only. The loss-of-residence limb is in neither LN 127/2026 nor LN 166/2026 — data not publicly available in legislation; verify at Gibraltar Finance Centre. PR 469/2026 does not mention HEPSS | HMGoG PR 466/2026 + 469/2026 + 682/2026 + 813/2026 + LN 166/2026 (parliament.gi) + LN 127/2026 and consolidated Qualifying (Category 2) Individuals Rules 2004 (gibraltarlaws.gov.gi) — https://www.parliament.gi/uploads/contents/papers_laid/2026/Residency%20Regulations%202026.pdf | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 1 | --visa (GI)`

Tirol: the Grundverkehrsbehörde is the Bezirksverwaltungsbehörde (§ 26 TGVG 1996); approval (§ 12) is granted only if the acquisition does not conflict with staatspolitische Interessen and serves a public economic, cultural or social interest (§ 13 Abs 1 lit c). Salzburg: Zustimmung by the Grundverkehrsbeauftragte(r), or the Grundverkehrskommission for farm or forest land (§§ 20 Abs 3, 24, 25 S.GVG 2023, LGBl 95/2022). Vorarlberg: the Grundverkehrs-Landeskommission decides for non-farm land (§§ 7, 8, 13 Abs 2 GVG). EU/EEA: Tirol equates EU/EEA nationals and Art 50 EUV residence-title holders with Austrians outright (§ 3 Abs 1 TGVG). Salzburg (§ 22 S.GVG, declaration required) and Vorarlberg (§ 3 GVG) exempt acquisitions made in exercise of EU/EEA freedoms including free movement of capital; in Vorarlberg the capital route needs EU/EEA residence. (2) The caps that do exist are nationality-neutral second-home caps. Tirol: new Freizeitwohnsitze may not be declared permissible once registered Freizeitwohnsitze plus those allowed by Flächenwidmungsplan designation exceed 8 v.H. of total dwellings per the last Gebäude- und Wohnungszählung (§ 13 Abs 5 lit a TROG 2022, version in force 7 Jul 2026 per LGBl 51/2026). GAZETTED AMENDMENT IN FORCE 1 Jan 2027: Besluit of 10 Sep 2026 amending the Besluit huurprijzen woonruimte (Staatsblad 2026 nr. 267, issued 18 Sep 2026; one sub-item, art. I part C under 2, later by koninklijk besluit together with the pending Huurcommissie bill) — (1) WOZ-prijsopslag: a home pulled back to 186 points by the WOZ-cap (WOZ points above 33% of the total) may add the euro difference between the maximum rent at its uncapped points and the 186-point maximum; (2) the 5 minus points for a home with no outdoor space at all are scrapped (ministry estimate: maximum rent up about EUR 34/mo); (3) higher location valuation for small rijksmonumenten; (4) minimum WOZ value in the WWS reinstated; no transitional law — on running contracts the rent can only grow toward the new maximum within the annual maximum increase percentages. - `2027-01-01 (WWS limbs 1-3 gazetted: Besluit 10 Sep 2026, Stb. 2026, 267, uitgegeven 18 Sep 2026; limbs 4-5 still pending) | rental | Wet betaalbare huur optimisation package to stem rental sell-off — ENACTED IN PART. Stb. 2026, 267 amends the Besluit huurprijzen woonruimte (Raad van State advies 12 Aug 2026, W04.26.00199/I; Tweede Kamer approved the voorhang 16 Jun 2026; signed by Minister VRO E. Boekholt-O'Sullivan, kabinet-Jetten) and is in force 1 Jan 2027 with no overgangsrecht (one leges sub-provision, art. I C.2, on a date set by KB): (1) WOZ-prijsopslag (new art. 8b Bhw): where the WOZ-cap applies (WOZ points limited to 33% of the total, capped home set to 186 pts) the maximum rent rises by the gap between the rent limit at the uncapped point total and at 186 pts, while the home stays at 186 pts and stays regulated (Toelichting example: 200 pts = +€96.11, max €1,324.18 at 2026 amounts); (2) the -5 pts for having no outdoor space at all deleted (Toelichting: max rent limit ~+€34); (3) homes up to 40 m2 in a rijksmonument: WOZ divided by €233 instead of €268 (2026), ~13% heavier WOZ points (Toelichting: ~€34-85/mo incl. the 35% monument surcharge); higher maxima apply in full at a new contract, existing tenancies only via the capped annual increase. PENDING, not law: (4) temporary contracts for all students — dropped from the besluit after motie-Leijen, to be regulated by formal law, internetconsultatie still to follow per Begroting VRO 2027 (15 Sep 2026); today only students moving to another municipality for study qualify; (5) tijdelijke nieuwbouwopslag middenhuur (10% for 20 yrs) extended 4 yrs to construction starts before 1 Jan 2032 (now before 1 Jan 2028) — announced Kamerbrief 20 Apr 2026 (Kamerstuk 27926 nr. 409), separate besluit, internetconsultatie and voorhang still to follow, must be in place before 1 Jan 2028. Formal Wet betaalbare huur evaluation to the Kamer by 1 Jul 2027. ### 🇸🇮 SI

- `2024-12-23 (NOT ENACTED — proposal stage only) | tax | Davek na stanovanjske nepremičnine (proposed annual tax on second and further homes) NOT ENACTED — it never advanced past the izhodišča the Government confirmed 23 Dec 2024 (1.45% of the GURS posplošena vrednost, first home the owner actually lives in fully exempt, revenue to the state budget; MF collected responses to 25 Jan 2025). It would NOT have replaced NUSZ — MF: "ne gre za predlog nepremičninskega davka, ki bi po svoji vlogi nadomestil trenutno veljavno obremenitev z nadomestilom za uporabo stavbnih zemljišč (NUSZ)" and "predvideva ukinitev davka od premoženja, ne pa tudi NUSZ". As of 21 Oct 2026 no such law exists and BOTH legacy charges remain payable: NUSZ (municipal) and the ZDO davek od premoženja, whose 2026 amounts were valorised by Pravilnik in Uradni list RS 105/25 of 15 Dec 2025 as amended by 109/25 of 22 Dec 2025 (in use from 23 Dec 2025). FURS: "Če stanovanjska površina stavbe (v kateri ima zavezanec ali njegovi ožji družinski člani stalno prebivališče) meri manj kot 160 m2, se davek ne odmeri" — that relief attaches to the owner's registered permanent home, so a second home or vikend IS assessed. Since ZDavNepr was annulled (US RS U-I-313/13-86 of 21 Mar 2014) Slovenia has lacked a COMPREHENSIVE property tax, not annual property taxation as such — FURS: the annulment "ponovno vzpostavilo prejšnje stanje obdavčitve nepremičnin". The proposing 15th Government (Golob) left office 4 Jun 2026 and the DZ appointed the 16th Government (Janša) the same day on a 2026-2030 coalition agreement, whose published housing points cover lower taxation of long-term residential rents, not a property tax; the Government said on 14 Oct 2026 that its omnibus development law (a referendum on it is pending) "ne uvaja novih davkov ali prispevkov" — re-verify any successor plan at MF gov.si before quoting a rate | MF gov.si + FURS + PISRS — `https://www.gov.si/novice/2024-12-23-vlada-potrdila-predlog-izhodisc-za-obdavcitev-premozenja-in-razbremenitev-dela` and `https://www.fu.gov.si/davki_in_druge_dajatve/podrocja/davek_od_premozenja` + `https://www.fu.gov.si/zivljenjski_dogodki_prebivalci/sem_lastnik_ali_uporabnik_nepremicnine` + `https://www.gov.si/novice/2025-01-10-zbiramo-odzive-na-izhodisca-o-davku-na-stanovanjske-nepremicnine` | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 2 | --tax`

- `2025-09-25 | rental | Budapest District VII (Erzsébetváros) two-stage STR crackdown. Building decree 33/2025 (IX.24), amending Építési Szabályzat 25/2018 (XII.21), in force 25 Sept 2025 (its § 17: day after promulgation): commercial accommodation in residential buildings, expressly including short-term letting, allowed only on max 10% of the building's residential usable floor area, on the ground floor only, with rooms of min 8 m² single / 12 m² double / +4 m² per extra bed and an en-suite bathroom (WC, washbasin, shower) per room; a panzió only with ≥6 rooms + breakfast room; non-business community/leisure accommodation barred from residential buildings. The decree has no transitional clause — per THT (secondary) it applies to new authorisation procedures, so operating units may continue. THEN decree 46/2025 (XII.10), in force 1 Jan 2026 and applied to pending cases: STR usable days/yr = 0 for magánszálláshely and egyéb szálláshely UNLESS a condominium (társasház) agreement exists (then 365) — a general-meeting resolution, SZMSZ provision or founding-deed clause clearly referring to the accommodation; fine HUF 200,000 (natural person / sole trader) or HUF 2,000,000 (legal person) or a 45-day closure, set without discretion; units registered by 31 Dec 2025 must obtain and file the agreement by 31 Dec 2026 — same zero-day mechanism as District VI (decree 26/2024 X.31, upheld by Kúria Köf.5.023/2025/4 of 11 Nov 2025), with a condo-consent carve-out. Budapest-wide moratorium (Kertv. 11/H, inserted by Act LVI of 2024, in force 1 Jan 2025; text in force from 1 Oct 2026 unchanged): no new magánszálláshely or egyéb szálláshely registered until 31 Dec 2026 (exception: classification request filed by 31 Dec 2024), and for units registered by 31 Dec 2024 no change of operator registered until 31 Dec 2026 | Erzsébetváros decrees 33/2025 + 46/2025 + consolidated 25/2018 via net.jogtar.hu + Kertv. 11/H via net.jogtar.hu + Kúria Köf.5.023/2025/4 — https://net.jogtar.hu/rendelet?council=erzsebetvaros&dbnum=560&docid=A2500046.07R | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 1 | --rental`

- `ongoing | rental | Queenstown Lakes District STVA — CORRECTION, the flat "25% rates uplift" is wrong: QLDC publishes TWO bands — "Operating under 180 days means your property will be rated as Mixed Use increasing your rates payments by 25-35%" and "Operating between 181 to 365 days means your property will be rated as Accommodation increasing your rates payments by 50-80%"; a Homestay is Mixed Use at 25-35%; rating changes take effect only from the next rating year "beginning 1st July". De-minimis is CUMULATIVE AVAILABILITY, not one block — rates are unaffected "unless you only make your property available for short-term visitors for up to 28 days", and "These 28 days can include multiple stays by different guests and refers to the total length of time the property is available to be rented" (the live page governs over any older QLDC factsheet wording). STVA means paying guests staying under 90 days; the same guests staying 90 days or more are tenants. The 90-night cap is ZONE-SPECIFIC, not district-wide: verified verbatim for Lower Density Suburban Residential std 7.5.18.1 "The total nights of occupation by paying guests on a site do not exceed a cumulative total of 90 nights per annum from the date of initial registration" and the same 90 nights in Medium Density Residential std 8.5.17 (PDP Decisions Version Jun 2023); in both chapters Residential Visitor Accommodation is Permitted (7.4.6 / 8.4.7A) and breaching one of its standards is Restricted Discretionary (RD) per the Non-compliance status column. QLDC publishes a separate standards sheet per zone and states the rules "will depend on which District Plan Zone your property lies within"; a parcel showing no zone on the PDP map is still under the Operative District Plan (ask the QLDC Duty Planner) — never assume 90 nights or RD without checking the parcel's zone on QLDC ePlan. Homestays (resident on site): in most zones no night limit but at most five paying guests per night; never permitted in the General Industrial & Service Zone. Registration is mandatory and "if you wish to operate beyond the permitted standards for your zone, then a resource consent will be required from QLDC before operating" | QLDC live STVA page `https://www.qldc.govt.nz/services/rates-property/short-term-visitor-accommodation` + PDP ch.7 Decisions Version Jun 2023 `https://www.qldc.govt.nz/media/k5yjp1kf/pdp-chapter-07-lower-density-suburban-residential-jun-2023.pdf` + PDP ch.8 Decisions Version Jun 2023 `https://www.qldc.govt.nz/media/yv5gq002/pdp-chapter-08-medium-density-residential-jun-2023.pdf` | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 1 | --rental`

CORRECTION: the effective field was 2024-10-22, an artefact of a superseded 22 Oct 2024 lower-house date; the gazette shows the lower-house vote on 3 Dec 2024. Covers FBiH + RS + Brčko (state-level, NOT FBiH-only). 40 m² buyer + 15 m² per family-household member; refund capped at 14.5299% (= 17/117, the VAT share of a 17%-VAT-inclusive price) × (BiH average flat price incl. VAT from the latest Agency for Statistics quarterly bulletin before the contract date) × 55 m². Only new builds whose sale is subject to VAT; buyer must not have owned a flat, house, apartment or other dwelling for living or letting in BiH in the 7 years before the claim date; 5-year no-resale lock-in from service of the decision (breach = repay the refund plus interest; art. 67 fine 5,000-10,000 KM). Claims due by 30 June of the year after the sale is registered; sales registered between 20/25 and 45/26 taking effect get 6 months from 8 Jul 2026 (computed ~8 Jan 2027); sales registered before 20/25 took effect are excluded. Claims go to the UINO regional centre for the seller's seat, decided within 6 months. ELIGIBILITY IS ADULT BiH CITIZENS WITH PERMANENT BiH RESIDENCE ONLY — foreign buyers cannot claim it. the same Act funds federal agencies to 11 Dec 2026, so appropriations and NFIP authority expire on the SAME day; no later extension found on 21 Oct 2026 (congress.gov H.R. 6500 latest action 2 Sep 2026; Holland & Knight Oct 2026 year-end-deadlines note still gives 11 Dec 2026); prior link H.R. 7148 = Consolidated Appropriations Act, 2026, Public Law 119-75 of 3 Feb 2026, which set the superseded 30 Sep 2026 date; standalone bills H.R. 5577 (Union Calendar 15 Jan 2026) and H.R. 7862 (referred 9 Mar 2026) are introduced, not enacted; on a lapse FEMA "would stop selling and renewing policies" while paying valid claims (FEMA's reauthorization page attributes the lapse impact estimate to the National Association of Realtors: ~1,300 property sales a day, ~40,000 closings a month — not a FEMA estimate), and CRS: "In past NFIP lapses, borrowers were not able to obtain flood insurance to close, renew, or increase loans secured by property in an SFHA until the NFIP was reauthorized" — in-force contracts run to the end of their one-year term; FEMA's Congressional Reauthorization page now shows 11 Dec 2026 ("Last updated September 28, 2026") after still showing 30 Sep 2026 on 17 Sep 2026 — it lagged the law by more than three weeks, so cross-check the live date on congress.gov or CRS IN10835; revisit set for the day after the 11 Dec double cliff | govinfo `https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/BILLS-119hr6500enr/html/BILLS-119hr6500enr.htm` + congress.gov H.R. 6500 + CRS IN10835 + FEMA `https://www.fema.gov/flood-insurance/rules-legislation/congressional-reauthorization` | 2026-10-21 | 2026-12-12 | 1 | --risks --insurance --finance (US)`

- `2017-06-01 | finance | AML Ley 155-17 (law of 1 Jun 2017; Reglamento de aplicación Decreto 408-17 of 16 Nov 2017) — the duties fall on the intermediaries, not on a buyer filing: agentes inmobiliarios and empresas constructoras become sujetos obligados no financieros when they intervene in compra y venta de bienes raíces for clients, buyers or sellers (DGII Norma General 03-2018 art. 6), and abogados / notarios / contadores when they transact for clients in compra, venta o remodelación de inmuebles (DGII Norma General 01-2018 art. 2); supervisor = DGII, reports go to the UAF. Each must take reasonable measures to identify the client, the beneficiario final of the transaction and the origen de los fondos (art. 5 g of both Normas); legal-person clients need identificación y verificación del beneficiario final (Norma 03-2018 art. 24); cash operations of US$15,000 or more are reported to the UAF as an RTE (art. 35); debida diligencia ampliada is mandatory for non-resident clients with cash operations above US$15,000 a day and for clients or transfers linked to high-risk jurisdictions (art. 27); records kept 10 yrs (art. 5 l); Ley 155-17 continuous due diligence includes, when necessary, documentation of the fuente u origen of the funds. Norma General 03-2022 repealed only art. 33 of Norma 03-2018. ### 🇨🇴 CO

- `2020 | finance | Decreto 1339/2020 MIS-CITED — that decree is MinAmbiente's designation of the legal representative of the Complejo de Páramos Las Hermosas (8 Oct 2020), nothing to do with cadastre. Catastro multipropósito rests on Ley 1955/2019 arts. 79-82 plus Decreto 1983/2019 and Decreto 148/2020, both regulating those articles inside Decreto 1170/2015 (catastromultiproposito.gov.co normatividad + Función Pública). Live event is the Ley 2294/2023 art. 49 one-off rezago adjustment: IGAC Resolución 2057 of 30 Dec 2025 reajusts, once, rural avalúos formed/updated before 2018 across 527 municipios from 1 Jan 2026; IGAC reported 520 municipios adjusted (23 Feb 2026) and states the avalúo adjustment is not an automatic or proportional predial increase; no follow-on art. 49 resolution for vigencia 2027 located 2026-10-21. 2-3x predial bills OVERSTATED — statutory ceilings are IPC+8pp if actualizado and max 50% if not, and 100% of IPC for estratos 1-2 viviendas up to 135 SMMLV (Ley 1995/2019 art. 2), plus max 2x the prior year after a formación catastral (Ley 44/1990 art. 6; IGAC cites it for rural predios of 100 ha or more); the Ley 1995 caps do NOT apply to terrenos urbanizables no urbanizados or urbanizados no edificados, lots whose new avalúo comes from construction, autoavalúo / autoestimación predios, changed destino económico or area/construction changes, where bigger jumps are lawful. - `2024-12-09 | tax | CORRECTED — Ecuador has NO holding-period test and NO habitual-residence test. LRTI art. 9 num. 14, as substituted by the Ley Orgánica para el Alivio Financiero y el Fortalecimiento Económico de las Generaciones en el Ecuador (5th supp. RO 699, 9 Dec 2024, in force on publication), exempts from income tax "Los generados por la enajenación ocasional de inmuebles de personas jurídicas o naturales. Esta disposición será aplicable hasta por dos (2) enajenaciones por año"; occasional = not part of the seller's ordinary business or habitual activity. RLRTI art. 18 (SRI codification): exempt gains include accessory parking / storage units when transferred in a single act, costs of the exempt sale are not deductible, and sales by businesses doing lotización / urbanización / construcción / promoción of real estate are habitual, not occasional. The same law repealed LODESF Disposición Transitoria Décimo Quinta (exemption for the first transfer of a property for five years after the Nov 2021 law). The superseded 2021 LODESF wording (RO 587 3S, 29 Nov 2021) limited the exemption to natural persons selling housing, its accessories and land. Natural persons running more than two real-estate projects lose the num. 14 exemption for the year in which they obtained an IVA refund (article added by the law in RO 461 S, 20 Dec 2023). A third or later sale in a year, or a habitual seller, is taxable; how the taxable gain is computed and the non-resident rate were not re-read in this pass — data not publicly available here, verify at SRI. Supersedes "0 % after 1-year holding (NOT 5 years)", "UNIQUE LATAM", "LRTI art. 8" and "Ley Orgánica de Reforma Tributaria" (the rule comes from the RO 699 law) | SRI LRTI codification art. 9 num. 14 https://www.sri.gob.ec/o/sri-portlet-biblioteca-alfresco-internet/descargar/12c884d5-c8d9-4171-becc-1513b990b87a/LEY_DE_REGIMEN_TRIBUTARIO_INTERNO_LRTI.pdf + SRI RLRTI codification art. 18 + RO 699 5S text | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 1 | --tax --exit`

unpermitted work costs GEL 2,000 on the employer per worker AND GEL 2,000 on the worker or self-employed foreigner (Art. 16¹), twice that on repeat, and three times the doubled fine for a further breach after a doubled or tripled fine, i.e. GEL 12,000 (computed: 3 x 2 x 2,000); employer procedure under the Res. №70 Rules as amended by Res. №321 of 9 Jul 2026 (in force on publication 10 Jul 2026): the vacancy-posting step was replaced by an employer annual quota — an employer planning more than 5 foreign nationals in a calendar year, or whose foreigners exceed 5% of headcount (unless 5% is fewer than 5 people), must agree a quota with the Agency at least 10 working days before applying, with Revenue Service proof of annual turnover of at least GEL 50,000 per foreigner (GEL 35,000 for educational or medical institutions); exempt from that procedure (Rules Art. 5(1)(ზ)): International Company status (Tax Code Art. 23) and innovative-startup status companies, NCEQE expert-corps international experts, or a vacancy that simultaneously pays over GEL 15,000 gross per month AND requires a relevant higher-education degree (the Agency may still apply the procedure, Art. 5(2)); Annex 3 annual sector quotas: courier 0, passenger transport 0, tourist guides 0, mountain/alpine/ski guides 200; Law №1509 of 15 Apr 2026 (in force on publication) took OUT of scope work done fully remotely for a local employer that needs no entry into Georgia (Art. 1(4)(j)) and work or services for a non-resident tied to that non-resident's activity outside Georgia (Art. 1(4)(k)), but the Res. №70 Rules (Art. 1(3)(ლ) as amended by Res. №321) withhold the non-resident exemption from an individual entrepreneur who needs a residence permit to stay lawfully, a carve-out not in the Law text; - `2021-02 | foreign-buyer | PP No. 18/2021 (ditetapkan and diundangkan 2 Feb 2021, LN 2021/28, in force on promulgation per Pasal 104; JDIH Kota Surabaya lists it as Berlaku with no status change recorded) — Hak Pakai (a right of use, not freehold) is the direct land right open to Orang Asing (Pasal 49(2)(e)); term on Tanah Negara / Tanah Hak Pengelolaan is 30 + 20 extension + 30 renewal = up to 80 yr (Pasal 52(1)), but Hak Pakai over Hak Milik is max 30 yr, renewable by a new akta pemberian hak pakai (Pasal 52(3)), which Pasal 53(3) requires to be made by a PPAT. Eligibility is a "dokumen keimigrasian" (Pasal 69(1)), which the Penjelasan defines as "visa, paspor, atau izin tinggal" — KITAS/KITAP is NOT a precondition in the PP text, and implementing Permen ATR/BPN 18/2021 Pasal 185 likewise asks only for a dokumen keimigrasian "yang diterbitkan oleh instansi yang berwenang" (which document a given Kantor Pertanahan accepts in practice — confirm there). Pasal 1 angka 14 defines Orang Asing as a non-citizen "yang keberadaannya memberikan manfaat, melakukan usaha, bekerja, atau berinvestasi di Indonesia", and Pasal 50 obliges a Hak Pakai holder who no longer meets Pasal 49 to release or transfer the right within 1 year, failing which it is extinguished by law. Foreigners may hold Hak Milik atas Satuan Rumah Susun (Pasal 67(1)(c): an Orang Asing "yang mempunyai izin sesuai dengan ketentuan peraturan perundang-undangan"; Permen 18/2021 Pasal 188(1)) in rumah susun on Hak Pakai OR HGB land (Pasal 71(1)(b)), limited by Pasal 71(2) to KEK, free-trade/free-port zones, kawasan industri and "kawasan ekonomi lainnya", which the Penjelasan defines broadly as "kawasan perkotaan dan/atau kawasan pendukung perkotaan, kawasan pariwisata, atau kawasan yang mendukung pembangunan hunian vertikal dan memberikan dampak ekonomi kepada masyarakat". Pasal 72 limits foreign holdings by minimum price, plot area, number of plots/units and residential use; Permen 18/2021 Pasal 186 sets them: a landed house must be in the rumah mewah category, 1 bidang tanah per orang/keluarga and/or at most 2,000 m2 (more plots or area only "dengan izin Menteri" where the holding has a positive economic and social impact), and a condo must be a rumah susun komersial; Pasal 187(2) leaves the price to a Keputusan Menteri (not read — verify at ATR/BPN). JDIH ATR/BPN lists Permen 18/2021 as Berlaku but dicabut sebagian by Permen ATR/BPN 2/2025 (provisions on changing hak milik into HGB or hak pakai, except in an auction); Permen 2/2025 was itself revoked by Permen ATR/BPN 5/2025 (diundangkan 28 Apr 2025), whose Pasal 20 routes a hak milik to HGB change through an auction or release to State land plus a new grant and does not mention hak pakai — whether the Pasal 187(3) change of Hak Milik or HGB into Hak Pakai on a foreign purchase still operates is data not publicly available — verify at the Kantor Pertanahan | PP 18/2021 official text via JDIH Kota Surabaya `https://jdih.surabaya.go.id/uploads/peraturan/3815_pp_18-2021.pdf` + Permen ATR/BPN 18/2021 via JDIH ATR/BPN `https://jdih.atrbpn.go.id/peraturan/detail/1030/peraturan-menteri-agraria-dan-tata-ruang-kepala-badan-pertanahan-nasional-nomor-18-tahun-2021` + Permen ATR/BPN 2/2025 status `https://jdih.atrbpn.go.id/peraturan/detail/1200/peraturan-menteri-agraria-dan-tata-ruang-kepala-badan-pertanahan-nasional-nomor-2-tahun-2025` | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 1 | --tax --visa ownership`
- `ongoing | foreign-buyer | Nominee ownership is void by statute, not merely risky — UU 5/1960 (UUPA) Art. 26(2), text as published on the Ministry ATR/BPN JDIH portal, makes any act intended to transfer hak milik to a foreigner directly or indirectly "adalah batal karena hukum dan tanahnya jatuh kepada Negara" (void by operation of law, and the land falls to the State), adding that "semua pembayaran yang telah diterima oleh pemilik tidak dapat dituntut kembali" (payments the owner has received cannot be reclaimed), with Art. 27(a)(4) extinguishing the hak milik outright — so the foreign payer's outlay is not secured by the land it bought. The statute's own elucidation confirms the legitimate route: "hanya warganegara Indonesia saja yang dapat mempunyai hak milik atas tanah … Orang-orang asing dapat mempunyai tanah dengan hak pakai yang luasnya terbatas". Kanwil BPN Provinsi Bali (statement of 7 Jul 2025 by Kakanwil I Made Daging) records ~463 Hak Pakai parcels held in WNA names Bali-wide (regulator's own wording "sekitar 463", on data through Mar 2025), each generally not exceeding 5,000 m2, and states nominee practice "bersifat tersembunyi dan umumnya baru dapat terungkap ketika muncul persoalan hukum" (hidden, generally surfacing only once a legal problem arises) because the paperwork formally sits in an Indonesian citizen's name. SUPERSEDES the prior wording: no Indonesian court is named "Bali Provincial Court" (Bali's general courts are Pengadilan Negeri Denpasar and Pengadilan Tinggi Denpasar), and the asserted 2023-2025 nominee voidings were never verified — the Direktori Putusan at putusan3.mahkamahagung.go.id returns HTTP 403 to automated retrieval (re-confirmed 2026-10-21), so any circulating 2023-2025 nominee case count is unverified and must be checked manually against the docket | Kementerian ATR/BPN JDIH UU 5/1960 `https://jdih.atrbpn.go.id/peraturan/download/32/UU_05_1960.pdf` + Kanwil BPN Provinsi Bali `https://bali.atrbpn.go.id/berita/kepala-kantor-wilayah-bpn-provinsi-bali-klarifikasi-terkait-isu-pulau-di-bali-yang-dikuasai-wna` (page body is JavaScript-rendered; same statement text read on the Kanwil's official Instagram account kanwil.bpn.bali, post of 7 Jul 2025 `https://www.instagram.com/p/DL1Q5gnTXAm`) | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 1 | --tax ownership`
- `2023-08-24 | visa | Second Home Visa (Visa Rumah Kedua, Imigrasi index E33) — CORRECTED, both thresholds are set in US dollars and the property route is condo-only: Permenkumham 22/2023 (in force 24 Aug 2023, BN 2023/651) Pasal 56(2) requires a Jaminan Keimigrasian of either (a) a commitment to keep at least US$130,000 "atau setara" in an account in the applicant's own name at a bank milik negara, or (b) a commitment to buy "properti berupa rumah susun atau apartemen di Indonesia senilai paling sedikit US$1.000.000 (satu juta dolar Amerika) atau setara", to be met within 90 days of the ITAS; Pasal 56(3) lets the Direktur Jenderal re-evaluate both values. The live Imigrasi E33 page (read 2026-10-21; legal basis listed as Permenkumham 22/2023 as amended by Permenkumham 11/2024 plus Kepmen Imigrasi dan Pemasyarakatan M.IP-08.GR.01.01/2025) states the same two options, proof reported to the issuing Kantor Imigrasi within 90 days of entry, the guarantee kept at or above the committed amount while the permit is valid, first permit up to 5 yr with total stay capped at 10 yr, PNBP Rp13,000,000 for up to 5 yr. So the property itself CAN be the qualifying asset, but only a rumah susun / apartemen worth at least US$1M — a landed house or villa on Hak Pakai is not named. - `2025-10-10 | rental | SUPERSEDED CITATION, not a substantive reversal — Permenparekraf 4/2021, which the ID playbook --rental headline rule cites (playbook line 332, grep-verified 2026-08-07) as the operative sectoral standard, has been revoked. Permenpar 6/2025 (Standar Kegiatan Usaha, Tata Cara Pelaksanaan Pengawasan, dan Sanksi Administratif pada Penyelenggaraan Perizinan Berusaha Berbasis Risiko Sektor Pariwisata) Pasal 22, in BAB VI Ketentuan Penutup, reads verbatim "... Nomor 4 Tahun 2021 tentang Standar Kegiatan Usaha pada Penyelenggaraan Perizinan Berusaha Berbasis Risiko Sektor Pariwisata (Berita Negara Republik Indonesia Tahun 2021 Nomor 283) dan ... Nomor 8 Tahun 2021 tentang Sanksi Administratif Perizinan Berusaha Berbasis Risiko Sektor Pariwisata dan Ekonomi Kreatif (Berita Negara Republik Indonesia Tahun 2021 Nomor 682), dicabut dan dinyatakan tidak berlaku", and Pasal 23 "Peraturan Menteri ini mulai berlaku pada tanggal diundangkan". The salinan carries "Ditetapkan di Jakarta pada tanggal 10 Oktober 2025"; its Diundangkan date did not render legibly in the copy read here, so the in-force date is taken from BPK JDIH, whose CATATAN reads "Peraturan Menteri Pariwisata ini mulai berlaku pada tanggal 10 Oktober 2025" (BN 2025 No. 793). WHERE THE STANDARD NOW SITS: in the national ministerial Lampiran, which carries "STANDAR KEGIATAN USAHA VILLA TINGKAT RISIKO MENENGAH RENDAH NO. KBLI: (55193) VILLA" and a parallel "NO. KBLI: (55130) PONDOK WISATA" standard, with compliance evidenced by a "Sertifikat Standar Usaha Villa Dengan Tingkat Risiko Menengah Rendah ... yang diberikan oleh Lembaga Sertifikasi Usaha/Lembaga Sertifikasi Produk Bidang Pariwisata", a third-party conformity body ("lembaga penilaian kesesuaian milik pihak ketiga"). This confirms from the primary text the KBLI 55193 villa code the ID block already uses at playbook line 336. It speaks to where the standard is SET, not to how it is enforced locally, about which this entry now makes no claim. TWO DATES TO DIARY: Pasal 5(2) requires the Sertifikat Standar Usaha Pariwisata "paling lambat 1 (satu) tahun terhitung sejak PB terverifikasi", Pasal 5(3) excepting certain "kegiatan usaha dengan tingkat Risiko menengah rendah tertentu"; and Pasal 21 gives operators whose Risk tier changed "jangka waktu paling lama 1 (satu) tahun terhitung sejak diundangkannya Peraturan Menteri ini", i.e. to 10 Oct 2026. The transitional article immediately preceding Pasal 21 keeps pre-existing OSS-issued PB and PB UMKU that were verified, approved and still valid before promulgation usable. WITHDRAWN — the prior wording of this line, that Bali villa STR enforcement "varies by kabupaten" and that Pondok Wisata licensing is "patchy", is not carried forward: no primary source is cited for it, and per-kabupaten STR enforcement activity and Pondok Wisata licence-issuance counts are data not publicly available — verify per property with the DPMPTSP and the Dinas Pariwisata of the specific kabupaten or kota where it sits, and against the OSS record for the business itself. DELIBERATELY NOT ASSERTED, in either direction: whether the legacy TDUP instrument survives the risk-based regime, whether Permenpar 6/2025 alters the nationality restriction on Pondok Wisata, and how the parent statute UU 10/2009 should now be cited. None was re-derived in this pass, so do not restate any of them on the strength of this entry. - `2025-07-01 | rental | Bali Foreign Tourist Levy IDR 150,000 cashless e-Payment only, amount unchanged since the 14 Feb 2024 launch and reconfirmed in Perda Bali 6/2023 Pasal 5(2) as amended by Perda Bali 2/2025 (ditetapkan + diundangkan Denpasar 20 Jun 2025, in force 1 Jul 2025, Lembaran Daerah 2025 No. 2, status Berlaku on peraturan.go.id). Levy is due on EACH entry to Bali "secara langsung dari luar negeri atau secara tidak langsung melalui wilayah lainnya di Indonesia" (Pasal 4A(1)), but one payment covers that visit: Pasal 5(1) runs "selama Wisatawan Asing berwisata ke Bali dan sebelum meninggalkan wilayah Indonesia" (carried over from old Pasal 5(2), not a 2025 change), and a duplicate paid on the same arrival date is refundable under Pergub 25/2025 Pasal 15A. Payment DEADLINE relaxed, not the pre-payment option — advance payment was always allowed: old Pasal 5(4) required payment "sebelum atau pada saat memasuki pintu kedatangan di Bali", new Pasal 5(3) allows "sebelum atau selama berwisata di Bali", and Pergub Bali 25/2025 Pasal 12(1) lists arrival by air-sea-land, during-stay, OR "keberangkatan Wisatawan Asing keluar Bali" (departure) as payment points. New Pasal 4A(2) EXEMPTS transport crew, KITAS/KITAP holders, diplomatic and service visas, family-reunification visas, student visas, golden visas, and "pemegang jenis visa lainnya" — an open catch-all with detail delegated to a Pergub by Pasal 4A(4), so treat the list as open, not closed; the KITAS/KITAP exemption is the material one for resident foreign owners. Sanctions RESTRUCTURED rather than tightened: old Pasal 6(2) flatly provided that non-payers "dilarang berwisata ke Bali"; new BAB VIIIA Pasal 16A substitutes graduated administrative sanctions (teguran lisan, teguran tertulis, and-or "tidak mendapatkan pelayanan di Daya Tarik Wisata"). Procedure is Pergub Bali 2/2024 ONLY as amended by Pergub Bali 25/2025 (ditetapkan 20 Jun 2025, in force 1 Jul 2025, Berita Daerah 2025 No. 28), which amends Pasal 1 angka 11-12, 4, 9(2), 12, 13, 14(1) and 14(4), 16(1), inserts Pasal 15A and BAB IVA Pasal 22A-22C Imbal Jasa capped "paling tinggi 3%" of levy transactions, and deletes BAB V and Pasal 23 — citing Perda 2/2025 Pasal II(1) alone points at superseded procedure text. No 2026 instrument superseding either found as at 2026-10-21: the Bali JDIH register (dashboard-jdih.baliprov.go.id API, terms pungutan and wisatawan asing) lists no later levy Perda or Pergub, the Perda 2/2025 record carries only its Mengubah link to Perda 6/2023 and the Pergub 25/2025 record no amending link, and lovebali.baliprov.go.id still states IDR 150,000 under Perda 2/2025 | Bali Provincial Govt JDIH via lovebali.baliprov.go.id (gubernur/v5/8.pdf Perda 2-2025 + gubernur/v5/9.pdf Pergub 25-2025 + gubernur/v4/8.BaliGovernorRegulation.pdf Perda 6-2023) cross-confirmed at peraturan.go.id and peraturan.bpk.go.id + `https://lovebali.baliprov.go.id/gubernur/v5/9.pdf` + Bali JDIH register `https://dashboard-jdih.baliprov.go.id/api/v1/laws?term=pungutan` | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 2 | --rental`
- `2026-02-24 | rental | Bali Perda 4/2026 tentang Pengendalian Alih Fungsi Lahan Produktif dan Larangan Alih Kepemilikan Lahan Secara Nomine — ditetapkan and diundangkan Denpasar 24 Feb 2026, in force on promulgation (Pasal 26), LD Provinsi Bali 2026 No. 4, signed Wayan Koster. WHAT IT PROHIBITS: Pasal 13(2) bars conversion of Lahan Produktif designated in the spatial plan (exceptions in 13(3) for national strategic policy, public-interest land acquisition and disaster relocation); Pasal 14 bars any person from acting as "perantara, fasilitator, atau menyediakan sarana yang menyebabkan warga negara asing menguasai kepemilikan lahan secara Nomine", Nomine being defined (Pasal 1 angka 11) as an arrangement naming someone to hold or manage land rights on behalf of a foreign national as beneficial owner. SANCTIONS ARE ADMINISTRATIVE: Pasal 20(2) lists written warning, temporary suspension, site closure, licence revocation or annulment, demolition, restoration of land function, incentive withdrawal and administrative fine; Pasal 20(5) adds only that criminal sanctions may apply "sesuai dengan ketentuan peraturan perundang-undangan". The Perda does not itself create a criminal offence or a prison term for nominee arrangements. THE 5-YEAR + IDR 1bn FIGURE is UU 41/2009 Pasal 72(1), for an individual converting designated LP2B land contrary to Pasal 44(1); UU 41/2009 appears in the Perda's Mengingat (legal basis), not in its sanctions article, and it is a land-conversion penalty, not a nominee penalty (corporate officers: Pasal 74, 2-7 yr + IDR 2-7bn). OTA NIB: the 31 Mar 2026 date was superseded — Kemenpar's own release says OTAs will require NIB + KBLI + NKU checked against OSS through an API targeted to launch June 2027; the Minister's 26 May 2026 press conference announced delisting of ~1,600 verified unlicensed operators from 1 Aug 2026 (secondary: ANTARA, Kontan, Media Indonesia); Kemenpar's own 26 May 2026 release gives no ~1,600 figure and no 1 Aug date; it says Kemenpar will send OTAs a list of unlicensed accommodation and the OTAs will delist within two months of that communication. Kemenpar's 20 Oct 2026 two-year release says it worked with nine OTAs on 'sosialisasi dan penerapan kebijakan termasuk delisting' and that short-stay accommodation units with an NIB reached 113,692 as at 23 Sep 2026, but it gives no count of listings delisted, so whether the ~1,600 delisting was carried out is still not verified. INVESTMENT LIST (corrected 2026-10-21): Perpres 10/2021 Lampiran II, as replaced by Perpres 49/2021 (ditetapkan 24 May 2021, diundangkan 25 May 2021, LN 2021 No. 128; Perpres 10/2021 status Berlaku and amended only by Perpres 49/2021 per JDIH Kemenko Infra), lists under item 48 Jasa Penginapan Hotel Bintang 1 (KBLI 55110), Hotel Melati (55120), Pondok Wisata (55130), Guest House (55199) and Vila (55193) as 'Dialokasikan untuk Koperasi dan UMKM', with the Kemitraan column blank. How OSS treats a PT PMA applying for KBLI 55193 was not verified — verify at BKPM (bkpm.go.id) and OSS (oss.go.id). Still NOT RE-VERIFIED from primary text: that Permenpar 18/2016 reserves Pondok Wisata to Indonesian citizens — data not publicly available in primary text read — verify at Kemenpar JDIH (jdih.kemenpar.go.id); the KBLI 55193 villa and 55130 pondok wisata standards themselves sit in Permenpar 6/2025, see that entry | JDIH Provinsi Bali — Perda 4/2026 signed salinan (dashboard-jdih.baliprov.go.id API record b0de5129-0933-454f-91b7-8de70934b5d7) + UU 41/2009 via peraturan.bpk.go.id/Download/28115 + Kemenpar siaran pers kemenpar.go.id/berita/siaran-pers-kemenpar-kembangkan-sistem-api-bersama-ota-perkuat-tata-kelola-akomodasi-pariwisata + (secondary) sumbar.antaranews.com/berita/763892 + Perpres 49/2021 Lampiran II via OSS `https://s3.oss.go.id/oss/cms/Lampiran-II-Peraturan-Presiden-Nomor-49-Tahun-2021-9168208c211b097945b476f6a4024ea1.pdf` + Kemenpar siaran pers kemenpar.go.id/berita/siaran-pers-pariwisata-tumbuh-positif-dalam-dua-tahun-pemerintahan-prabowo-gibran | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 1 | --rental`

MYR 1.5M condo: foreigner MYR 120,000 vs citizen/PR MYR 44,000 (computed), ~2.7x or ~173% more (was 60k vs 44k, ~36% more, at the old 4%). LHDN response to the CTIM/MIA/MICPA/MAICSA joint memorandum on Budget 2026 and Tax Bills 2025: 32(ab) applies to SPAs signed from 1 Jan 2026 (an SPA of 20 Dec 2025 with an MOT of 5 Jan 2026 is not caught by the 8%); joint ownership is charged per owner's share (citizen/PR share at 32(a), non-citizen share at 32(ab)); foreign company takes the Companies Act 2016 meaning (body incorporated outside Malaysia). - `ongoing (IGOC 2026 in force 2026-01-01) | foreign-buyer | Morocco exchange control — CORRECTED: what secures repatriation is the convertibility regime (garantie de transfert) for investments financed in foreign currency, not an investor 'registration' with the Office des Changes, and skipping it does NOT lock rental income or sale proceeds in dirhams outright. IGOC 2026 (Office des Changes, in force 1 Jan 2026): foreign investments, including acquisition of immovables and construction works, financed in foreign currency (transfer from abroad, debit of a foreign-currency or convertible-dirham account; art. 173) get free transfer of income, of sale or liquidation proceeds and of proceeds from succession; the proof is the bank's settlement justificatif (e.g. Formule 2 currency purchase, Formule 4 convertible-dirham debit, avis de crédit), which the bank must issue and copy to the client — the foreign-investment chapter sets out no separate investor filing with the Office des Changes. Without foreign-currency financing: (a) rental and other investment income — banks may pay it to non-resident foreign persons without limit in amount or time whatever the financing mode; resident foreigners holding 10+ years without proof of FX financing may transfer the last closed year's income up to MAD 2,000,000 a year; (b) sale proceeds — net dirham proceeds (after taxes) go to a seller resident in Morocco, or into a compte convertible à terme for a non-resident foreign seller, transferable in four equal 25% tranches (first on credit, the rest on each anniversary; arts. 175 and 241); (c) sales between foreign nationals or to a Moroccan resident abroad may be settled directly abroad, the buyer inheriting the seller's convertibility status. For a sale-proceeds transfer the bank requires the original settlement justificatifs plus copies of the acte d'achat and acte de vente and proof of taxes paid | Office des Changes IGOC 2026 (foreign-investment chapter + investment-income section + comptes convertibles à terme) — https://www.oc.gov.ma/sites/default/files/reglementation/pdf/2026-01/IGOC%202026.pdf | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 1 | --tax --currency`

GAFI's live application form publishes five Decree-3099/2019 options: USD 250,000 non-refundable "direct revenue (grant) to Public Treasury Authority"; USD 500,000 state-owned real estate; USD 400,000 project at min 40% capital; USD 1,000,000 refundable after 3 years; USD 750,000 refundable after 5 years (both refunded in Egyptian pounds at the CBE rate on the refund date, capped at the deposit-date rate, without interest). - `2026-07-29 | tax | Real-estate disposal tax: 2.5% of the GROSS disposal value with no deductions — not a tax on net gain, so a loss-making sale is still taxed (computed from the statutory base). CORRECTION: the rule sits in Income Tax Law 91/2005 Art. 42 (2.5% on gross revenues from disposals already in the 2005 text, MoF translation) — NOT Law 175/2023 (GAFI lists 175/2023 as an income-tax amendment; no source seen ties it to this tax). Law 151/2026 (Official Gazette No. 30 bis (A), 28 Jul 2026, in force 29 Jul 2026) replaced the article: 2.5% without any deduction on the total disposal value of real estate or building land, villages excepted, however many disposals and whether registered or not; an heir disposing of inherited property is now expressly taxed (the 2005 text exempted heirs); the base is the value stated in the contract, the Tax Authority bearing the burden of proof if it disputes it; the disposer pays within 60 days of the disposal; in-kind contributions to joint-stock company capital held 5 years, compulsory sales, expropriation and gifts to the state fall outside the tax. The gazetted text heads the article Art. 43; NPI Egypt (tax adviser, secondary) reports a corrigendum in Official Gazette No. 32 bis of 8 Aug 2026 renumbering it Art. 42 — corrigendum not seen on a primary page. Whether the first-home carve-out (EGP 3M / under 200 m²) survives the replacement text — data not publicly available — verify at ETA | ETA-hosted Official Gazette text of Law 151/2026 https://eta.gov.eg/sites/default/files/2026-08/law.no_.151.of_.2026.pdf + Income Tax Law 91/2005 Art. 42 (MoF translation, WIPO Lex) + GAFI Financial Laws list + NPI Egypt (secondary) | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 2 | --tax --exit`

WITHDRAWN 1: the label 'Form 4' for any Egyptian foreign-property-purchase document — unsupported at every source tier reached across three verification passes; no replacement document name is asserted here — data not publicly available — verify with the Ministry of Justice Real Estate Publicity Department. WITHDRAWN 2: 'without it future USD repatriation of sale proceeds structurally blocked, sellers stuck with EGP-only exit' — no primary source states this, and the nearest primary statement points the other way: US State Dept ICS 2025 Egypt (Remittance Policies) 'The 1992 U.S.-Egypt Bilateral Investment Treaty provides for free transfer of dividends, royalties, compensation for expropriation, payments arising out of an investment dispute, contract payments, and proceeds from sales' (a US-investor treaty right, not a general rule for all nationalities). RE-ATTRIBUTED from CBE to the Ministry of Justice (Real Estate Publicity Department), which GAFI names as the 'Relevant government agency'. ESTABLISHED ONLY AS AT 13 Nov 2024, the Last-Modified date of the GAFI FDI Regulations PDF (header re-checked 2026-08-07): 'A law is currently under review to amend certain provisions of Law No. 230 of 1996. The amendment aims to allow non-Egyptians to own an unlimited number of properties in Egypt, eliminating the previous restriction of two properties in two different cities, provided payment is made in foreign currency'; the same page records that the Council of Ministers approved the amendment 'in session No. 246 on July 5, 2023' and that 'The Ministry of Justice has completed the drafting of this amendment to be presented to the Egyptian Parliament'. WHETHER IT HAS SINCE BEEN ENACTED — data not publicly available — verify at the Egyptian Official Gazette and the House of Representatives record before relying either on the two-property cap or on an unlimited-ownership foreign-currency route. Egyptian press additionally reports a PM Decision No. 2021 of 2026 delegating the Prime Minister's Article (2) and (5) powers to the Minister of Justice and repealing PM Decision No. 2725 of 2024 — not seen in the Gazette by this pass, and GAFI's own legal-basis column already records two earlier delegations of those same powers (PM Decree 686 of 2013, PM Decree 1237 of 2018), so a further delegation does not on its own establish that the ownership cap moved. ALSO UNPROVEN: a notarisation and registration gate requiring evidence the price was wired from abroad in foreign currency to a CBE-authorised bank, attributed to REPA Technical Publication 8 and Circular 41 of 2024, is described only by law firms and Egyptian press, the instruments being internal and ungazetted — data not publicly available — verify in writing with the Ministry of Justice Real Estate Publicity Department before contracting | GAFI FDI Regulations (Last-Modified 13 Nov 2024) https://www.gafi.gov.eg/English/Documents/FDI%20Regulations.pdf + US State Dept Investment Climate Statement 2025 Egypt https://www.state.gov/reports/2025-investment-climate-statements/egypt/ ; PM Decision 2021 of 2026 is press-reported only and unverified against the Official Gazette | 2026-08-07 | 2027-02-03 | 3 | --tax --currency --finance --exit --compare --notary --agent --retirement ownership`
- `ongoing (Law 14/2012; Sharm El Sheikh / Dahab / Gulf of Aqaba regime in force 2022-04-01) | foreign-buyer | Sinai — no freehold for foreigners, usufruct only, under two regimes. (1) Most of Sinai, Law 14/2012 on Integrated Development of Sinai (MIFT consolidated text): with approval of the National Authority for the Development of Sinai board plus Defence, Interior and General Intelligence, non-Egyptians may own built structures only, never the land, and may hold usufruct of residential units for at most 50 years (Art. 2); usufruct contracts run up to 50 years, renewable to a 75-year total with board approval, and never convert to ownership (Art. 3); military-strategic areas, border-adjacent areas (Presidential Decree 444/2014), Red Sea islands, nature reserves and antiquities sites are closed to any disposal; contracts without the approvals are absolutely void. (2) CORRECTION for Sharm El Sheikh, Dahab and the Gulf of Aqaba tourism sector: Presidential Decree 128/2022 (issued 23 Mar 2022, in force 1 Apr 2022) takes them out of Law 14/2012 — ownership of land AND built real estate is reserved to Egyptian nationals and wholly Egyptian-owned companies; state land is granted by usufruct of up to 75 years in total (not 50), the grantee owning the buildings it erects during the term, and buildings may be sold without the land; every residential allocation, usufruct grant or disposal, for Egyptians or foreigners, needs Defence, Interior and General Intelligence approval or the contract is absolutely void; non-Egyptian investment projects must be Egyptian joint-stock companies. The granting body is the competent land authority (the decree says جهات الولاية and does not name TDA) — the prior wording, leasehold from TDA, is not established. No source quantifies how often sales offices market Sinai units as freehold; skill guidance: treat any freehold label on a Sinai unit offered to a foreigner as a deal-killer red flag until the contract type and the security approvals are seen | Law 14/2012 consolidated text (MIFT) https://mift.gov.eg/legislation/download/299 + Presidential Decree 128/2022, Government Printing Office text http://alamiria.com/Sec/TashTxt?id=37B2gztNoYs%3D | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 1 | --tax ownership`

### 🇲🇺 MU

- `2026-07-01 | tax | Finance Act 2025 (Act 18/2025) §49 RDA s.3(1G) + §29 LDTA s.4(9) had raised non-citizen EDB-scheme / NCPRA s.3(3)(c)(v) residential registration duty (buyer) + resale land transfer tax (seller) to 10% for transfers on/after 1 Jul 2026 — REPEALED by Finance Act 2026 (Act 14/2026; passed 31 Jul, assented 12 Aug, Gazette 13 Aug 2026 per the National Assembly Acts 2026 table): enacted s.16(b) repeals RDA s.3(1G) and s.16(e)(i) repeals RDA First Schedule Part I paragraph K; enacted s.9(a)(i) repeals LDTA s.4(9) (now confirmed in the gazetted Act text, no longer Bill-only); standard 5% registration duty (purchaser) + 5% land transfer tax (vendor) applies (EDB). Enacted s.28 (Commencement) gives ss.9/16 no specific or deemed date (Constitution s.46(4): no law operates before Gazette publication; EY lists the LDTA limb effective 12 Aug 2026 and the RDA repeal's first year of application as 1 Jul 2026; KPMG lists the repeal effective 12 Aug 2026), so treatment of deeds registered 1 Jul–12 Aug 2026 = data not publicly available — verify at MRA / Registrar-General. MU stays CGT-free (the 30%-of-gain formula was never enacted: FA 2025 Seventh Schedule Part III set a flat 10 per cent). NEW (FA 2026 s.9(a)(ii)+(d), LDTA s.4(11) + Seventh Schedule Part IV, rate 10 per cent): additional duty, paid by the transferor, on any deed witnessing the transfer to a non-citizen of residential property on State land or Pas Géométriques under NCPRA s.3(3)(c)(v); not applicable where a presale agreement (VEFA contrat de réservation préliminaire or promesse de vente) was executed before 19 Jun 2026 and drawn up and signed before a notary. Separately ANNOUNCED in Budget 2026-27 (EDB highlights), not in Act 14/2026: Government will no longer grant leases authorising the sale of apartments on State land / Pas Géométriques and will cease such sales to non-citizens (leases approved before 19 Jun 2026 and disposals by existing owners excepted) | Finance Act 2026 (Act 14 of 2026) ss.9 + 16 + 28 — lawsofmauritius.govmu.org + mauritiusassembly.govmu.org; National Assembly Acts 2026 table; Finance Bill No. XII of 2026; EY Alert on Finance Act 2026 (17 Sep 2026); KPMG Tax Alert Issue 09 (Aug 2026); EDB Getting Started + EDB Budget Highlights 2026-27; Finance Act 2025 Act 18/2025 §29 + §49 — https://mauritiusassembly.govmu.org/mauritiusassembly/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/Act1426.pdf | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 1 | --tax ownership --visa`
- `2025-07-01 | tax | PIT scale 0/10/20% took effect for the income year commencing 1 Jul 2025 (Finance Act 2025) and is still the scale MRA administers as at 2026-08-07 — the MRA PAYE table reads First Rs 500,000 at 0%, Next Rs 500,000 at 10%, Remainder 20%, and employees with monthly emoluments not exceeding Rs 38,462 are not subject to PAYE (company director and board fees excepted). CORRECTION to this entry's prior wording: the scale did NOT replace a "15% flat" rate. MRA's own Budget 2025/26 Fiscal Measures Highlights prints the outgoing scale beside the new one as ELEVEN bands — First Rs 390,000 at 0%, then 2%, 4%, 6%, 8%, 10%, 12%, 14%, 16%, 18%, remainder 20% — and states "The new income tax rates and bands will be effective as from income year starting on 01st July 2025". That eleven-band scale is the FA-2023 one effective from 1 Jul 2023, whose MRA communique of 08 Jun 2023 also records "The solidarity levy has been abolished". Budget Speech 2025-2026 para 261: "Regarding personal income tax, we are reducing the tax bands from eleven to three". ALSO LIVE: Fair Share Contribution — MRA states an individual pays FSC "in addition to his liability to income tax where his income threshold exceeds Rs 12 million in an income year", collected under PAYE "as from 01 July 2025 ... up to 30 June 2028", the employer withholding 15% of monthly emoluments exceeding Rs 923,077 (Rs 12,000,000 divided by 13), so the top marginal load exceeds 20%. NOT ESTABLISHED: Budget Speech 2026-2027 para 280 announces a further restructure — 20% on the portion of chargeable income "exceeding Rs 1 million up to Rs 12 million", 35% above Rs 12 million, and "this tax band will replace the fair share contribution for individuals" — but whether that measure is enacted or in force for the income year commencing 1 Jul 2026 is data not publicly available — verify at MRA and the Attorney-General's statute portal before relying on either scale for income year 2026-27. - `2025-08-09 (Finance Act 2025 s.13, Gazette No. 66) | visa | Retired Non-Citizen Residence Permit threshold raised USD 1,500/mo → USD 2,000/mo: Finance Act 2025 (Act 18/2025, assented 8 Aug 2025, Gazette No. 66 of 9 Aug 2025) s.13(d) replaced the EDB Act First Schedule — Part III (Criteria for Residence Permit, retired non-citizen) now requires an initial transfer of at least USD 2,000 into a local bank within 60 days of issue of the permit, thereafter USD 24,000 annually or USD 2,000 per month (previously USD 1,500 initial, then USD 1,500/mo or USD 18,000/yr); s.13 is not listed in the Act's s.66 Commencement, so it operated from Gazette publication. Part IV item 4 (PRP for a retired non-citizen) now requires 5 years as RP holder + aggregate transfers of at least USD 200,000 over the 5 years before application. The residency.mu EDB page (age 50+, 10-year RP renewable, USD 24,000 or USD 2,000/mo bank-statement test) matches; the passport.govmu.org Occupation/Residence Permit guideline PDF (2020 file) still shows the superseded USD 1,500/mo or USD 18,000/yr, and the PIO Occupation Permit page still shows the superseded PRP test (3 years + USD 54,000) | Finance Act 2025 s.13(d) + EDB Act First Schedule Parts III-IV — https://mauritiusassembly.govmu.org/mauritiusassembly/wp-content/uploads/2025/08/18_THE-FINANCE-ACT-2025-.pdf; EDB — https://residency.mu/retire/retired-non-citizen-50-years; Act 13 of 2026 s.15 | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 2 | --visa`
- `2024-12-13 | foreign-buyer | 85/15 currency rule IN FORCE since 13 Dec 2024 (the Feb 2025 date is only the EDB FAQ explainer's own stamp, not the amendments' date). EDB (Smart City Scheme / Property Development Scheme / Invest Hotel Scheme / Real Estate Development Scheme) (Amendment No. 2) Regulations 2024 (Cabinet took note 6 Dec 2024, in force 13 Dec 2024) require a non-citizen acquiring residential property under IRS/RES/IHS/PDS/SCS, after transferring funds from abroad in hard convertible currency, to pay 85% of the purchase price to the promoter in Mauritius rupee with the remaining 15% in USD/EUR/any hard convertible FX or MUR; the notary receives the price in FX and effects the 85/15 split on transfer to the promoter (the bank applies the exchange rate on the transfer date instructed by notary and promoter, FAQ Q9); registration duty is payable in USD or other hard convertible FX (FAQ Q10). Local-loan floor raised USD 500,000 → USD 750,000: up to USD 750,000 the acquisition is financed from own funds transferred from abroad; where the price exceeds USD 750,000 the first USD 750,000 must be own funds transferred to Mauritius and paid in MUR to the promoter, with a Mauritian bank loan (may be contracted in MUR) only for the balance and loan repayment in hard convertible FX. Per EDB FAQ the amendments apply to a first sale only (resales out of scope) and NOT to Ground+2 apartments. Transition (FAQ Q5): the 85/15 rule applies to sales deeds signed from 13 Dec 2024 and does NOT apply to deeds signed before 13 Dec 2024 even where VEFA tranche payments fall due on or after that date; the USD 750,000 loan rule applies to EDB letters of authorisation (LOAs) issued from 13 Dec 2024 and to valid earlier LOAs where the sales deed was not yet signed. Resident carve-out (FAQ Q13/Q16/Q17): a non-citizen holding an Occupation Permit / retired or other Residence Permit may use funds previously transferred from abroad (on proof of transfer, subject to conditions), and one earning MUR income in Mauritius may finance the whole acquisition in MUR; residents may still borrow locally only above the first USD 750,000 of own funds. No repealing or amending regulation found (EDB newsroom notice 20 Dec 2024 + FAQ still published 2026-10-21; Finance Act 2026 (Act 14) and Economic and Financial Measures (Miscellaneous Provisions) Act 2026 (Act 13) contain no change to the schemes' payment rules). NB the 85% forced-MUR leg imposes a real FX-spread cost on the full purchase price for buyers funding from abroad. | EDB Mauritius (FAQ-Amendments document Feb 2025 + newsroom notice 20 Dec 2024) + https://edbmauritius.org/wp-content/uploads/2025/03/FAQ-Amendments-to-Property-Regulations.pdf + https://edbmauritius.org/newsroom/amendments-to-irs-res-ihs-pds-and-scs-regulations | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 2 | --tax ownership --visa`
